- **tcp-sack-rfc2018**: RFC 2018 — TCP Selective Acknowledgment Options reference. SACK lets receivers report non-contiguous received blocks so senders retransmit only missing segments. Two options — SACK-Permitted (Kind=4, SYN-only) and SACK (Kind=5, left/right edge pairs, max 4 blocks). Receiver behavior (first block = triggering segment, include max blocks, repeat for robustness across ACK loss). Sender scoreboard (SACKed flag, skip during retransmit, clear all on RTO due to possible reneging, dequeue on cumulative ACK only). Reneging permitted but discouraged. Congestion control preserved. No extra logic. 4. **COMMIT** — `git commit -m "feat: implement &lt;behaviour&gt;"` 5. **REFACTOR** — Improve code quality without changing behaviour. ([chukaofili/agent-plugins](https://github.com/chukaofili/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/coding-protocol/skills/tdd))
- **tdd**: Comprehensive guide for Test-Driven Development (TDD) methodology. Use this skill when the user asks to implement features using TDD, write tests first, follow red-green-refactor cycle, or develop code with test-first approach. Also use when user mentions TDD, unit testing workflow, or wants to refactor code with test coverage. ([chuyeow/human-general-intelligence](https://github.com/chuyeow/human-general-intelligence/tree/HEAD/plugins/human-general-intelligence/skills/tdd))
- **tdd**: TDD deprecated shim ([Ciward/oh-ciward-codex](https://github.com/Ciward/oh-ciward-codex/tree/HEAD/skills/tdd) · 3 copies)
- **tdd**: Test-driven development with red-green-refactor loops built around vertical slices (tracer bullets), not horizontal layers. Use when asked to "use TDD", "write test-first", "red-green-refactor", "build this with tests", or whenever a feature has a clear observable contract and would benefit from tests that outlive refactors. Complements the bundled test and add-tests skills — use this for the discipline, use those for the mechanics. ([cmetech/otto-cli](https://github.com/cmetech/otto-cli/tree/HEAD/src/resources/skills/tdd) · 2 copies)
- **tdd**: Test-first development against a ticket's Done-when lines, one line at a time (red, green, next). Use when implementing or fixing a ticket, or when asked to work test-first. ([cmsecchi/punchlist](https://github.com/cmsecchi/punchlist/tree/HEAD/skills/tdd))
- **tdd**: テスト駆動開発（TDD）で機能を実装する。Red→Green→Refactorのサイクルを厳守し、各ステップでコミットする ([cocoeyes02/tdd-vibe-coding-php85](https://github.com/cocoeyes02/tdd-vibe-coding-php85/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/tdd))
- **tdd**: Red-green-refactor loop. Use when implementing logic or fixing a bug that can be reproduced in a test. ([cod3rocket/agent-skills](https://github.com/cod3rocket/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/rocket/skills/tdd))
- **tdd**: Implement features and bug fixes in small red-green slices using behavior-focused tests at existing public seams.
